float ComputeDiffractionWeight(int dx, int dy, float kernelRadius) {
    float dist = sqrt((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
    // Avoid division by zero.
    if(dist < 1e-4)
        return 1.0;
    float x = PI * (dist / kernelRadius);
    float sinc = sin(x) / x;
    return sinc * sinc;
}



float CalcCurvature(
    Texture2D<float> depthMap, // Depth map texture.
    float2 uv, // Texture coordinates.
    float2 texelSize, // Reciprocal of the texture dimensions.
    bool invertDepth, // Depth inversion flag.
    bool useProjectedDepth // Projected depth flag.
)
{
    // Sample the central depth.
    float center = depth2D(depthMap, uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    
    // Sample neighboring depths.
    float left = depth2D(depthMap, uv - float2(texelSize.x, 0.0), invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    float right = depth2D(depthMap, uv + float2(texelSize.x, 0.0), invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    float up = depth2D(depthMap, uv + float2(0.0, texelSize.y), invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    float down = depth2D(depthMap, uv - float2(0.0, texelSize.y), invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    
    // Calculate the Laplacian, which approximates the curvature.
    float laplacian = left + right + up + down - 4.0 * center;
    return laplacian;
}


// Computes the optical path length (OPL) phase contribution.
Complex3 ComputeOPLPhase(Complex3 cAngularFrequency, Complex3 cRefractiveIndex, float depth) {
    // depth is converted to a complex signal (with offset if needed)
    Complex3 opL = ComplexMul(cRefractiveIndex, ComplexV0(depth));
    return ComplexMul(ComplexDiv(cAngularFrequency, ComplexV0(SPEED_OF_LIGHT)), opL);
}

// Computes the Fresnel phase contribution.
Complex3 ComputeFresnelPhase(float3 R0, C3 viewDir, C3 normal, C3 fresnelFactor) {
    C3 fresnel = CMul(CAdd(CV0(R0),CSub(CV0(1.0), CV0(R0))), CPow(CSub(CV0(1.0), CSat(ComplexDot3(viewDir, normal))), CMax(CV0(EPSILON), CV0(FresnelPower))));
    return CMul(fresnel, fresnelFactor);
}





float2 ToPolar(float2 uv)
{
    float r = length(uv);
    float theta = atan2(uv.y, uv.x);
    return float2(r, theta);
}

Complex3 TranscendentInterferenceComplex(
    Texture2D<float4> diffuseMap,
    Texture2D<float> depthMap,
    float2 uv,
    float3 viewPos,
    float time,
    bool invertDepth,
    bool useProjectedDepth)
{
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Universal Coordinate Remapping
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Map the UV coordinates from screen space into a centered, universal frame.
   
 uv = (uv / GetSz_4(diffuseMap)) * 2.0 - 1.0;
    uv= uv * 0.5 + 0.5;

    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Sample Scene Data
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float4 diffuse = diffuse2D(diffuseMap, uv);
    float depth0 = depth2D(depthMap, uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    // Wrap the scalar depth value into a Complex3 and offset by PhaseOffsetG.
    Complex3 depth = ComplexAdd(
                            ComplexAdd(ComplexV0(depth0), ComplexV0(-0.5)),
                        ComplexV0(0.5)
                    );
    float3 normal = CalcNormal2(depthMap, uv, viewPos, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth, NormalRadius);
    
    float3x3 tbn = CalcTBN(ComplexV0(normal));


    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Establish a Fixed, Cosmic Reference
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // A universal axis to anchor our interference beyond local orientation.
    float3 cosmicAxis = mul(tbn, normalize(float3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0) + viewPos));

    // Derive polar coordinates from uv (representing angular parameters)
    float2 polar = ToPolar(uv);

    // Compute base wavelengths (in nm) from the diffuse color,
    // and modulate them by depth. Now lifted to Complex3.
    Complex3 cWavelengths = RGBToWavelengthsNM(ComplexV0(diffuse.rgb));
    // Multiply by (1.0 + depth * 0.05) – using Complex arithmetic.
    cWavelengths = ComplexMul(cWavelengths,
                               ComplexAdd(ComplexV0(1.0), ComplexMul(depth, ComplexV0(0.05))));

    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Initialize the Hyperdimensional Signal
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Complex3 signal;
    signal.real = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    signal.imag = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);

    const int iterations = 4;
    float basePhase = 6.28318530718; // 2π constant

    // Pre-wrap constants used in the loop.
    Complex3 one = ComplexV0(1.0);
    Complex3 cMinWavelengths = ComplexV0(MIN_WAVELENGTHS);
    Complex3 cWavelengthRange = ComplexV0(WAVELENGTH_RANGES);
    // Compute wavelength scale factor: ((wavelengths - MIN_WAVELENGTHS) / WAVELENGTH_RANGES)
    Complex3 wavelengthScale = ComplexDiv(ComplexSub(cWavelengths, cMinWavelengths), cWavelengthRange);

    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Accumulate Hyperwave Interference
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    [unroll(4)]
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++)
    {
        // --- Temporal Phase Blending ---
        // Original: t = depth.real + depth.imag * (i * 0.1 * HeightScale)
        // Lift into Complex3: note that ComplexV0(depth.real) wraps the real part.
        Complex3 cT = ComplexCreate(depth.real,
                                  (depth.imag*(float(i) * 0.1 * HeightScale)));

        // --- Hyper-Space Position Computation ---
        // Base spatial position: combine UV and depth.
        Complex3 basePos = ComplexV0(float3(uv, depth.real.x));
        // Phase factor from iteration and temporal blend:

        // (1.0 - cT)
        Complex3 diffT = ComplexSub(C10, cT);
        Complex3 phaseFactor = ComplexAdd(CV0(i*0.1), diffT);
        // Multiply to get primary positional contribution.
        Complex3 posPart = ComplexMul(phaseFactor, basePos);

        // Normal contribution.
        Complex3 normalContribution = ComplexMul(ComplexV0(normal), ComplexV0(float(i) * 0.01));
        // Cosmic (TBN-anchored) contribution.
        Complex3 cosmicContribution = ComplexMul(ComplexV0(cosmicAxis), ComplexV0(float(i) * 0.01));
        Complex3 diffuseContribution = ComplexMul(ComplexV0(diffuse.xyz), ComplexV0(float(i) * 0.01));

        // Sum the spatial contributions.
        Complex3 posSum = CAdd(diffuseContribution ,ComplexAdd(ComplexAdd(posPart, normalContribution), cosmicContribution));
        // Apply the wavelength scaling.
        Complex3 pos = ComplexMul(posSum, wavelengthScale);

        // --- Compose Hyperphase ---
        // Wrap the angular component: extend polar (float2) to float3 with a fixed 1.0.
        Complex3 cPolar = ComplexV0(float3(polar, 1.0));
        // Multiply basePhase * cPolar.
        Complex3 phaseMult = ComplexMulScalar(cPolar, basePhase);
        // Combine with the temporal blend cT.
        Complex3 multiplier = ComplexMul(phaseMult, cT);
        // Final hyperphase: pos * multiplier.
        Complex3 cPhase = ComplexMul(pos, multiplier);

        // Compute the hyper-exponential interference wave.
        Complex3 wave = ComplexExp(cPhase);

        // --- Noise & Parallax Modulation ---
        // Use the xy of the real part of pos as sample coordinates.
        Complex3 modSample = ComplexV0(noiseMap1.SampleLevel(sampleTypeLinear, pos.real.xy, 0).xyz);
        Complex3 mod = ComplexMul(ComplexMulCf(modSample, ParallaxFactorB), depth);

        // Modulate the wave and accumulate.
        wave = ComplexMul(wave, mod);
        signal = ComplexAdd(signal, wave);
    }

    // Return the final, normalized interference pattern.
    return signal;
}


Complex3 InterferenceComplex2(Texture2D<float3> noiseMap, Texture2D<float> depthMap, float2 uv, float3 viewPos, float time, bool invertDepth, bool useProjectedDepth)
{
    // Transform fragment coordinates to centered space
    uv = (uv / GetSz_4(diffuseMap)) * 2.0 - 1.0;
    float2 sampleUV = uv * 0.5 + 0.5;
    
    // Sample scene channels
    float3 diffuse = noise2D(noiseMap, uv);
  
    
    float depth0 = depth2D(depthMap, uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    Complex3 depth = CV0(depth0);
    float3 normal = CalcNormal2(depthMap, sampleUV, viewPos, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth, NormalRadius);

    // Derive polar coordinates from uv
    float2 polar = ToPolar(uv);
    
    // Compute wavelength multipliers from diffuse channels and depth.
    // These modulate the interference fields per color channel.
    Complex3 wavelengths = RGBToWavelengthsNM(ComplexV0(diffuse));
 
    Complex3 signal;
    signal.real = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    signal.imag = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    
    const int iterations = 8;
    float basePhase = 6.28318530718; // 2π

    float3x3 tbn = CalcTBN(ComplexV0(normal));
    float3 polar2 = mul(tbn, float3(polar, 1.0));
    
   [unroll(4)]
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++)
    {
        C3 t = CAdd(CV0(depth.real),  CV0(depth.imag * (cos(time)+i)* HeightScale));
    
    // Compute pos in screen- or object-space (without tbn rotation)
        float3 pos = (i * 0.1 + (1.0 - ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(t)).real)) * float3(sampleUV, ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(depth)).real.x) *
                 ((ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(wavelengths)).real - MIN_WAVELENGTHS) / WAVELENGTH_RANGES);
    
    // Use the original polar coordinates (embedded in a float3) without tbn rotation.
    // Here, we replace polar2 with a fixed coordinate: float3(polar, 1.0)
        float3 phase = pos * (basePhase * float3(polar, 1.0) * ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(t)).real * ((ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(wavelengths)).real - MIN_WAVELENGTHS) / WAVELENGTH_RANGES));
    
    // Compute the interference wave in Complex space
        Complex3 wave = ComplexExp(ComplexV0(phase));
    
    // Get modulation factors from noise and other parameters (unchanged)
        float3 mod = noiseMap1.SampleLevel(sampleTypeMirror, sampleUV, 0).xyz * ParallaxFactorA *
                 ComplexSaturate(ComplexAbs(depth)).real;
       
    // Modulate and accumulate the wave
        wave = ComplexMulCf(wave, mod);
        signal = ComplexAdd(signal, wave);
    }

    return signal;
    
}



// Accumulate contributions over a square kernel.
// This function returns a weighted complex sum and the total weight.
void AccumulateInterference(
    Texture2D<float3> noiseMap,
    Texture2D<float> depthMap,
    Texture2D<float4> diffuseMap,
    float2 uv,
    bool invertDepth,
    bool useProjectedDepth,
    float AnimateTime,
    bool xrayMode,
    float offsetScalarI, float offsetScalarR, float offsetDepth,
    out Complex3 accum,
    out Complex3 weightSum)
{
    accum = ComplexV0(0.0);
    weightSum = ComplexV0(0.0);
    int halfKernel = 4;
    float2 oosz = GetOosz(depthMap);
    float2 ooszD = GetOosz(diffuseMap);
    
    // Loop over the kernel.
    for (int i = -halfKernel; i <= halfKernel; i++) {
        for (int j = -halfKernel; j <= halfKernel; j++) {
            float2 sampleUV = uv + float2(i, j) * oosz;
            float2 sampleUVD = uv + float2(i, j) * ooszD;
            // Sample depth and compute corresponding phase.
            float sampleDepth = depth2D(depthMap, sampleUV, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
            C3 sampleDepthC = CAdd(CAdd(CSub(CV0(sampleDepth),CV0(0.5)), CV0(offsetDepth)), CV0(0.5)); // Apply offset if needed.
            
           
	    C3 fastOffset = CAdd(CMul(CSin(CMul(CAdd(CSub(CV0(float3(sampleUV,offsetDepth)),CV0(0.5)), CCos(CV0(AnimateTime))),CV0(offsetScalarI))) ,CV0(offsetScalarR)),CV0(0.5));

            Complex3 phaseSample = CAdd(ComplexExp(CSub(fastOffset,CV0(0.5))), CV0(0.5));
            // Incorporate noise modulation.
            float3 noiseValue = noiseMap.Sample(sampleTypeMirror, sampleUV);
            phaseSample = ComplexAdd(phaseSample, ComplexCreate(noiseValue * 0.1, -noiseValue * 0.1));
            
            // Compute diffraction weight.
            float w = ComputeDiffractionWeight(i, j, halfKernel*2);
            
            // Accumulate weighted contribution.
            accum = ComplexAdd(accum, ComplexMulScalar(phaseSample, w));
            weightSum = ComplexAddCf(weightSum, w);
        }
    }
}

// A simplified interference function using modular phase computations.
Complex3 InterferenceHolographic_Modular(
    Texture2D<float3> noiseMap, 
    Texture2D<float> depthMap, 
    Texture2D<float4> diffuseMap, 
    float2 uv, 
    float2 deltaUV,
    bool invertDepth, 
    bool useProjectedDepth, 
    float3 lightPos, 
    float3 viewPos,
    float depthScale, 
    float AnimateTime, 
    bool xrayMode,
    float centralWeight,
float offsetScalarI,
float offsetScalarR, float offsetDepth)
{
    // Sample depth and adjust UV.
    float2 adjustedUV = uv + deltaUV;
    float depth0 = depth2D(depthMap, adjustedUV, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    // Create a Complex3 depth signal (offset can be adjusted if needed).
    Complex3 depth = ComplexAdd(ComplexV0(depth0), ComplexV0(offsetDepth));

    // Compute surface normal and TBN matrix.
    float3 normal = CalcNormal2(depthMap, adjustedUV, viewPos, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth, NormalRadius);
    float3x3 tbn = CalcTBN(ComplexV0(normal));
    float3 pixelPos = float3(adjustedUV, depth0);
    float3 viewDir = safeNormalizef(viewPos - pixelPos);

    // Retrieve albedo from the diffuse map.
    float3 albedo = diffuse2D(diffuseMap, adjustedUV).rgb;
    // Convert albedo to effective wavelengths (in nm), then to meters.
    Complex3 wavelengthsNM = RGBToWavelengthsNM(ComplexV0(albedo));
    Complex3 wavelengthsM  = nmToM(wavelengthsNM);

    // Compute frequency and angular frequency.
    Complex3 frequency        = ComplexDiv(ComplexV0(SPEED_OF_LIGHT), wavelengthsM);
    Complex3 angularFrequency = ComplexMulScalar(frequency, 2.0 * PI);

    // Material properties (assumed precomputed from a material function).
    MaterialSellmeier mat = CreateMaterial(MaterialIndex);

    // Compute separate phase contributions.
    Complex3 oplPhase = ComputeOPLPhase(angularFrequency, ComplexV0(mat.etaR), depth0);
    Complex3 fresnelPhase = ComputeFresnelPhase(mat.metallicReflectance, CV0(viewDir), CV0(normal), CV0(PI));
    
    // Curvature phase: sample curvature from depth map.
    float3 curvature = ComputeCurvature(depthMap, adjustedUV, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    Complex3 curvaturePhase = ComplexV0(curvature); // scaling factor can be adjusted

    // Fast offset phase: an animated offset based on UV.
    float2 fastOffset = sin(((adjustedUV-0.5) + cos(AnimateTime)) * offsetScalarI) * offsetScalarR + 0.5;
    Complex3 fastOffsetComplex = CAdd(ComplexV0(float3(fastOffset-0.5, offsetDepth)), CV0(0.5));

    // Combine phase contributions.
    Complex3 totalPhase = ComplexAdd(oplPhase, ComplexAdd(fresnelPhase, ComplexAdd(curvaturePhase, fastOffsetComplex)));
    Complex3 centralPhase = ComplexExp(totalPhase);

    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Diffraction Weighting Accumulation over a Kernel.
    //--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    int halfKernel = 4;
    
    Complex3 accum = ComplexMulScalar(centralPhase, centralWeight);
    Complex3 weightSum = ComplexV0(centralWeight);
    
    // Retrieve texture offset sizes for depth and diffuse maps.
    float2 oosz  = GetOosz(depthMap);
    float2 ooszD = GetOosz(diffuseMap);
    
    for (int i = -halfKernel; i <= halfKernel; i++) {
        for (int j = -halfKernel; j <= halfKernel; j++) {
            // Offset the UV for the current kernel sample.
            float2 sampleUV = adjustedUV + float2(i, j) * oosz;
            
            // Optionally, you may re-sample depth and recompute local phase variations.
            float sampleDepth = depth2D(depthMap, sampleUV, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
            float3 sampleCurvature = ComputeCurvature(depthMap, sampleUV, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
          
             C3 sampleFastOffset = CAdd(CMul(CSin(CMul(CAdd(CSub(CV0(float3(sampleUV,offsetDepth)),CV0(0.5)), CCos(CV0(AnimateTime))),CV0(offsetScalarI))) ,CV0(offsetScalarR)),CV0(0.5));
  
            Complex3 sampleCurvaturePhase = ComplexV0(sampleCurvature);
         
            
            // For this sample, reuse the central OPL and Fresnel phases (or recompute if needed).
            // Here we adjust the total phase slightly using local curvature and offset variations.
            Complex3 sampleTotalPhase = CAdd(totalPhase, CAdd(sampleCurvaturePhase, sampleFastOffset));
            Complex3 samplePhase = CExp(sampleTotalPhase);
            
            // Compute the diffraction weight for this kernel offset.
            float weight = ComputeDiffractionWeight(i, j, (float)(halfKernel*2));
            
            // Accumulate the weighted sample.
            accum = ComplexAdd(accum, ComplexMulScalar(samplePhase, weight));
            weightSum = ComplexAddCf(weightSum, weight);
        }
    }
    
    // Normalize the accumulated interference.
    Complex3 finalPhase = ComplexDiv(accum, weightSum);
    
    return finalPhase;
}

psout PS(PS_INPUT input)
{
    psout output;
    InitPSOut(output, input.uv);

    // Time-dependent values.
    float time = TotalTime*AnimateSpeed;
    bool xrayMode = !KeyEDown;
    bool invertDepth = KeyQDown;
    bool useProjectedDepth = !KeyWDown;
    float curvatureFactor = f9;
    float centralWeight = f8;
float offsetScalarI = f1;
float offsetScalarR = f2;
float offsetDepth = f3;
float depthScale = DepthScale;

    MaterialSellmeier mat = CreateMaterial(MaterialIndex);
    // Sample raw depth at the input UV.
    float depth0 = depth2D(depthMap, input.uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    float3 viewPos = float3(ViewX,ViewY,ViewZ);
    
    // Compute the surface normal and the tangent-bitangent-normal (TBN) matrix.
//    float3 normal = CalcNormal2(depthMap, input.uv, viewPos, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth, NormalRadius);

    Complex3x3 tbn = CalcTBN2(depthMap, input.uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    C3 normal = tbn.normal;

    // Compute the pixel's world position using the depth.
    float3 pixelPos = float3(input.uv, depth0);
    float3 lightPos = float3(SunX,SunY,SunZ);
    // Compute the view direction vector.
    C3 viewDir = CSafeNormalize(CSub(CV0(viewPos), CV0(pixelPos)));

    // Retrieve diffuse albedo from the diffuse map.
    float3 albedo = diffuse2D(diffuseMap, input.uv).rgb;

    // Convert albedo to effective wavelengths (in nanometers), then to meters.
    Complex3 wavelengthsNM = RGBToWavelengthsNM(ComplexV0(albedo));
    Complex3 wavelengthsM  = nmToM(wavelengthsNM);

    // Compute frequency and angular frequency.
    Complex3 frequency          = ComplexDiv(ComplexV0(SPEED_OF_LIGHT), wavelengthsM);
    Complex3 angularFrequency   = ComplexMul(frequency, CV0(f5*2.0 * 3.14159265));

    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Phase Contributions (Modularized)
    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Optical Path Length (OPL) phase.
    Complex3 oplPhase = ComputeOPLPhase(angularFrequency, CV0(mat.etaR), depth0);

    // 2. Fresnel phase (using a Schlick approximation).
    //    Note: 'mat' is assumed to be set elsewhere (e.g., via CreateMaterial(MaterialIndex)).
    C3 fresnelPhaseFactor = CV0(PI * (xrayMode ? 1.5 : 1.0) * FresnelPower);

    Complex3 fresnelPhase = ComputeFresnelPhase(mat.metallicReflectance*FresnelReflectance, viewDir, normal, fresnelPhaseFactor);

    // 3. Curvature phase.
    float3 curvature = ComputeCurvature(depthMap, input.uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth);
    Complex3 curvaturePhase = ComplexV0(curvature * curvatureFactor);

    // 4. Fast offset phase from animated offsets.
    Complex3 fastOffset = CAdd(CMul(ComplexSin(CMul(CAdd(CSub(CV0(float3(input.uv,offsetDepth)),CV0(0.5)), CCos(CV0(time))),CV0(offsetScalarI))) ,CV0(offsetScalarR)),CV0(0.5));
  

    // Combine phase contributions.
    Complex3 totalPhase = ComplexAdd(oplPhase, ComplexAdd(fresnelPhase, ComplexAdd(curvaturePhase, fastOffset)));
    Complex3 phase = ComplexExp(totalPhase);


    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Diffraction and Interference Accumulation via a Kernel
    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Start with the central contribution.
    Complex3 accum = ComplexMulScalar(phase, centralWeight);
    Complex3 weightSum = ComplexV0(centralWeight);

    // Accumulate contributions over a kernel neighborhood.
    Complex3 kernelAccum, kernelWeight;
    AccumulateInterference(noiseMap1, depthMap, diffuseMap, input.uv, invertDepth, useProjectedDepth, time, offsetScalarI, offsetScalarR, offsetDepth, xrayMode, kernelAccum, kernelWeight);

    accum = CAdd(accum, kernelAccum);
    weightSum = CAdd(weightSum, kernelWeight);

    // Normalize the accumulated complex signal.
    accum = CDiv(accum, weightSum);

    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Final Color Conversion and Gamma Correction
    //------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // For display purposes, convert the complex interference signal into a color.
    // Here we extract the magnitude as an example; you could encode phase as color too.
    float3 finalColor = ComplexToFloat3(CMul(accum, ComplexDot3(normal, viewDir)));
    finalColor = AdjustGamma(finalColor, Gamma);

    output.rt1 = float4(finalColor, 1.0);
	if(input.uv.x>0.5){
output.rt1.xyz = InterferenceHolographic_Modular(noiseMap1, 
    depthMap, 
    diffuseMap, 
    input.uv, 
    float2(0,0),
    invertDepth, 
    useProjectedDepth, 
    lightPos, 
    viewPos,
    depthScale, 
    AnimateTime, 
    xrayMode,
    centralWeight,
offsetScalarI,
offsetScalarR, offsetDepth);
}
    return output;
}
